待验证假设

  • The returns increase may be concentrated in a small number of stock codes (M and several numeric codes with zero baseline), consistent with specific product or cancellation events rather than broad-based return-rate drift.
  • The United Kingdom gross_sales increase may reflect a mix of higher order volume and larger basket size; sales_orders and avg_order_value comparisons would be needed to separate these.
  • The EIRE gross_sales decline may offset part of the United Kingdom gain; a country-level order-count check could test whether this is volume or price driven.
  • Returns rising faster than gross sales could reflect timing of cancellations booked in October against September sales; invoice-level timing analysis would be needed.

WITH periods AS (SELECT 'baseline' period, ?::TIMESTAMP a, ?::TIMESTAMP b UNION ALL SELECT 'current', ?::TIMESTAMP, ?::TIMESTAMP)
      SELECT p.period, coalesce(sum(quantity::DECIMAL(18,0) * unit_price),0)::VARCHAR value, count(DISTINCT CASE WHEN quantity > 0 AND NOT starts_with(upper(invoice_no),'C') THEN invoice_no END)::VARCHAR orders
      FROM periods p LEFT JOIN facts f ON invoice_time >= p.a AND invoice_time < p.b AND (unit_price > 0 AND NOT (starts_with(upper(invoice_no),'C') AND quantity >= 0)) GROUP BY p.period ORDER BY p.period

SQL、绑定参数与原始结果
SELECT country dimension, coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN quantity > 0 AND NOT starts_with(upper(invoice_no),'C') THEN quantity::DECIMAL(18,0) * unit_price ELSE 0 END),0)::VARCHAR value, CASE WHEN invoice_time < ?::TIMESTAMP THEN 'baseline' ELSE 'current' END period
      FROM facts WHERE (unit_price > 0 AND NOT (starts_with(upper(invoice_no),'C') AND quantity >= 0)) AND ((invoice_time >= ?::TIMESTAMP AND invoice_time < ?::TIMESTAMP) OR (invoice_time >= ?::TIMESTAMP AND invoice_time < ?::TIMESTAMP)) GROUP BY dimension, period ORDER BY dimension, period

SQL、绑定参数与原始结果
SELECT stock_code dimension, coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN quantity < 0 THEN -(quantity::DECIMAL(18,0) * unit_price) ELSE 0 END),0)::VARCHAR value, CASE WHEN invoice_time < ?::TIMESTAMP THEN 'baseline' ELSE 'current' END period
      FROM facts WHERE (unit_price > 0 AND NOT (starts_with(upper(invoice_no),'C') AND quantity >= 0)) AND ((invoice_time >= ?::TIMESTAMP AND invoice_time < ?::TIMESTAMP) OR (invoice_time >= ?::TIMESTAMP AND invoice_time < ?::TIMESTAMP)) GROUP BY dimension, period ORDER BY dimension, period
